## C. Per-family lens blocks (Phases 5–7, 10–12)

### G1

### C1 · Topbar
- **Tiers (code, observed):** PRIMARY — CTA (SH-24), save pill (SH-15) · SECONDARY — Exit, site name, preview, comments, issues chip, bell, ⋯ · ADVANCED — review pill, live chip · CONTEXTUAL — presence + connection pill (flag), review bar (open round) · RARE — compact-width drop. **Figma disagrees:** no Comments, Issues chip, live chip, presence, review bar; adds Search field (⌘K) + explicit Save button in the unsaved state (4418:125436) + History link.
- **Discovery:** every control is visible; Comments/Issues/Live discoverable in code only. Entry = shell load; return = n/a. Icon clarity: Figma's status *dot* clicks into History (unexpected — a dot reads as passive). Depth 1. Duplicated entry points: live URL (chip + menu), review (pill + bar + menu), publish (CTA + panel button). Could-be-contextual: live chip (only when live — already), review bar (fold into chip).
- **Navigation (dump):** btn/publish is CONDITIONAL on 42 shells (gate/panel) — consistent; btn/preview routes per page (3 conditionals). status/dot HOVER → 7184:76318 is not a live board (tooltip). No dead ends observed in G1 families.
- **Conditional UX:** flag on → presence; live → chip; round open → bar; errors > 0 → chip severity; offline → pill beats all (code). Figma has none of these conditions on the master except the publish gate and `hasDetachedComments`.
- **Consistency:** "Save" (Figma button) vs pill-click-saves (code); "Publish" (Figma) vs verb machine (code); History reachable by dot, link, ⌘K, menu.

### C2 · Site menu
- **Tiers:** PRIMARY — Site settings, Publish/Review doors · SECONDARY — Export, Share preview, Unpublish, view mode · ADVANCED — Templates/Components/Brand/Plugins doors, Copy live URL · CONTEXTUAL — Unpublish (live + admin), Start collaboration (flag) · RARE — Getting started, Keyboard shortcuts, dashboard links. Figma: 13 rows, no panel doors, adds Duplicate site + Activity log + Issues.
- **Discovery:** ⋯ is the least-discoverable door; in code it holds 7 panel doors that already have letters and ⌘K rows. Depth 1–2. Duplicates: G1-016.
- **Navigation:** 4418:126034 dismisses by click-outside (4611:45013) and Esc; "Site settings" fires CLOSE + SV + NAV; "View live site ↗" is a URL action; "Invite/Account" route via the 4418:124664 interstitial (extra hop).
- **Conditional UX:** code trims for Viewer and hides Unpublish unless live+admin, Start collaboration unless flag-on; Figma menu is static.
- **Consistency:** "Export site…" (Figma) vs "Export code" (code); "Activity log" opens dashboard (code) vs drawer (Figma).

### C3 · ⌘K / keyboard / cheat sheet
- **Tiers:** PRIMARY — GO TO / NAVIGATE rows · SECONDARY — actions (undo · preview · zoom) · ADVANCED — engine registry commands · CONTEXTUAL — disabled-with-reason rows, RECENT · RARE — Ask AI fallback. Figma adds JUMP TO entities and a stock-photo fallback.
- **Discovery:** ⌘K printed in the Figma search field; code has no visible search field (keyboard-only + menu row). Two palettes in code (⌘K, ⌘⇧P) with overlapping Edit/View groups. Two help screens (⌘/ and `?`).

### G2

### C1 · Canvas

**Control tiers (from code rows; Figma disagreements noted)**
- PRIMARY: click-select (CI-55), hover outline (CV-26), selection pill Duplicate/Delete/⋯ (CV-42), drag-move (CI-44), drop feedback (CV-62), inline text edit (CV-58), breakpoint switcher (CV-13), zoom (CV-16…19), undo/redo (CV-83). Figma agrees (`5936:44788`, `4428:139921`, `4428:140088`).
- SECONDARY: section reorder handles (CV-67), empty-page CTA (CV-22), marquee (CV-56), Shift-click (CI-56), resize handles (CV-34), ⋯ context menu (CI-01), snap guides (CV-68 — default on). Figma: guides are toggles only.
- ADVANCED: rotation (CV-36), Alt/Alt+Shift hover (CV-28/29), ⌘-click cycling (CI-57), rulers + guides (CV-70/71), grid (CV-73), spacing spots (CV-74), X-Ray/Badges (CV-77/78), ⌘⇧P (CV-88), keyboard nudge/reorder (CI-62). Figma places the overlay toggles in a View menu (`5930:44801`) — "low-frequency overlays grouped" (`5773:45444` copy) — which matches this tier.
- CONTEXTUAL: locked/dimmed looks (CV-03/04), CMS badge (CV-38), compact label while resizing (CV-53), device frame (Mobile/Tablet only, CV-20), pick-element crosshair (CV-98), read-only canvas (CV-101).
- RARE: Watch device (CV-15), Component view (CV-79), Inspector mode (CV-32 dead), touch drag (CI-50). Recommend delete/fold (G2-015, G2-036, G2-005, G2-029).

**Discovery** — Hover/selection are learned by pointing; the ⋯ menu is the only "what else can I do here" affordance in Figma and it is on the pill (right-click cannot be prototyped). Entry to overlays: code = footer word buttons (always visible, 6 of them); Figma = View menu (one control). Return: none needed (toggles). Icons: Figma device chips are icons + "M" (Q7); code uses letters W/D/T/M. Depth: overlays 1 click (code) vs 2 (Figma menu) — acceptable for ADVANCED. Duplicated entries: three ancestor pickers (G2-026), two zoom step rules (G2-016), two palettes (G2-038), two shortcut references (G2-039), two "more" menus (G2-024). Could-be-contextual: device frame toggle should only exist on Mobile/Tablet (already so in code) — Figma has none.

**Navigation (feature-level)** — Figma: the ⌘K "Replace layout with template…" row jumps to a confirm (`4418:54271`) without the choose step; the Inspector pick button lands in Assets pick mode (`6764:59051`); STATES · Canvas launcher `4418:142106` cards point at non-live hosts. Code: after "Start blank" the Add drawer opens (CV-23) — Figma `4428:44164` "Start blank" navigates to the Layers shell (`4418:81300`), not Add. Everything else round-trips.

**Conditional UX** — Selection → pill: single only (multi → compact label, code) — Figma shows no multi-select canvas state. Resize → toolbar hides, W×H label shows (code); Figma card only. Drag over illegal target → red badge + reason (code 10 reasons; Figma 1 sample). Empty page → CTA A; after Start blank → CTA B (code) vs Figma one board. Read-only → handles/pill hidden, comment pinning stays (code) vs Figma viewer shell (G1). Locked element → toast (code) vs inspector Unlock (Figma). Error/success: drop success is silent (code) vs toast+Undo (Figma) — owner decision E-1.

**Consistency** — Wrap in Container (toolbar) vs Wrap in section (menu) vs Group (⌘G): three wrappers → one (G2-052). "Insert" (pill "Add child element" modal title) vs "Add" (rail): the modal keeps the old library name (G2-116). Delete: instant + Undo toast (canvas/Layers keys) vs confirm modal (inspector ⋯, Figma everywhere) — E-2. Zoom: footer/cheat sheet "⌘1" vs ⌘K "Ctrl+0" (01 B-duplicates note; G1 confirms the ⌘K string). Toast tones: toolbar `info` vs menu `success` for the same action (CI-94). Two device selectors (footer + inspector pill) → one (G2-013). Figma's own breakpoint board mixes "375px" (menu) and "390 × 250" (status) on `4428:140088`.

**Tiers** — PRIMARY: Duplicate, Delete, Copy/Cut/Paste (CI-09…13). SECONDARY: Move up/down (CI-19), Wrap (CI-16), Replace with block… (CI-23), Save as component (CI-27), Add interaction (CI-26). Style ▸ has no destinations at all (`7052:78361`: only close reactions) — a dead end inside an overlay, though the 09-15 page-wide scan counts overlays as terminal (report, not re-scan).

**Conditional UX** — Root element: no Lock/Save/Wrap rows (code). Multi-select at open: Group row appears, but right-click on a non-primary member collapses the selection first (CI-30) — fix. Paste (element and styles) disabled until the clipboard holds something (CI-11/21) ✓ Figma greyed rows not evident (inf.). Refused insert → silent (CI-14/15) — Figma has no insert rows, so the problem disappears with G2-052.

**Consistency** — Figma ⋯ menu = Inspector ⋯ (`6918:73322`) + Pro mode row; code has three different "more" menus (toolbar dropdown, context menu, inspector ⋯). Shortcut hints: code prints them (Mac glyphs / "CtrlAltC" bug), Figma prints none. "Copy style" (Figma) vs "Copy styles" (code) — copy.

SECONDARY: master screen (AD-36), update from selection (AD-39), delete (AD-40), detach instance (AD-41/46). ADVANCED: per-page usage (Figma), detach all (Figma), edit master from instance (Figma), reset to master (IN-26). RARE: duplicate master (AD-38), variant chips (AD-42 stub), rename/select-variant modals (dead), export/import (dead), DS catalogue grid (dead).

**Discovery** — Entry (v3 Q4): Add › SAVED COMPONENTS "Manage components ›" (`4418:99857`), ⇧A, ⌘K "Open Components", Brand summary rows; canvas ⋯ "Save as component". Return: "‹ Add" / "‹ Saved components" drill-in headers (both). Icons: ◇ instance glyph (both). Depth: Add → Components → master = 3 (Figma) — acceptable; code: Components tab → detail = 2. Duplicated: Detach (Layers row · inspector · master "Detach all"); Create (panel footer · canvas ⋯ · Save-as-component row · AI "Save as component" verb). Could-be-contextual: "Update from selection" only when an element is selected (code disables, Figma always shows — inf.).

**Navigation** — Figma: master ⋯ actions → confirms → outcome boards → back to list ✓; properties board `4418:144558` is an annotated dead state (by design). Code: Insert from detail does not return to the list (AD-37 note); rename impossible (AD-47).

**Conditional UX** — 0 components → empty + Create (both). No IndexedDB → "Components not available" (code only). Update with instances → confirm with count + override-loss warning (code) / "Update the master?" (Figma) ✓. Delete with instances → confirm (both; Figma copy "18 instances become independent"). Detach → confirm (both). Instance selected + Pro mode → Detach row (code) — Figma always shows Detach on the row.

**Consistency** — Pro mode gates a structural action (detach) in code; Figma exposes it plainly. Create form: 6 fields (code) vs 2 + scope (Figma). "n on this site" count (both ✓). Thumbnails: Figma always, code never.

### C8 · AI

ADVANCED: whole-site / all-like-this scope (AD-60), Generate a block (Draft), Edit plan (Figma only), Keep N changes (Figma only). CONTEXTUAL: publish confirm (AD-63, flag), multi-select guard (AD-57), inspector takeover (IN-06). Return: "‹ Inspector" (both). Icons: ✦ everywhere ✓. Depth: 1. Duplicated: two AI surfaces (panel + popover) — deliberate, documented on `6881:86111`; two placements in code (drawer + inspector) — not deliberate. Could-be-contextual: "Improve with AI" hidden when unconfigured (code) — show and route to the Connect-provider state instead.

**Navigation** — Figma: every AI state returns to `4418:123573` or the inspector; Approval → "‹ Inspector" lands on AI pending `4418:172567` (a loose page-level board with one reaction — UNVERIFIED as intended). Code: none missing.

**Conditional UX** — Not configured / quota / timeout → full-panel states (both; copy differs). Multi-select → guard (both; Figma board contradicts itself). Step needs approval → pause with Skip/Approve (both). Step fails → Undo all · Retry (code) vs Undo all · Edit prompt · Keep 1 (Figma). Stop → stopped panel (both). Publish proposed → confirm (both, flag). Nothing to apply → third outcome (code only).

**Consistency** — Two conversation models in code (chat vs run) vs one in Figma (E-8). Counter "N left today" (Figma) has no source. Copy: "Open workspace settings" (code) vs "View workspace owner ↗" (Figma). Quota sentence relayed from the server (code) vs literal (Figma sample) ✓ shape.

Section throws → error card + Try again (both). Template just applied → banner (code) / ref (Figma). AI run → takeover (both).

Fill/Hug live in Size (code) but in the Display menu (Figma `6964:80863`). Corner radius: own section (code) vs inside Border (Figma). "Custom attributes · Soon" (Figma) vs shipped (code IN-92). ### C1 · Assets drawer

- **Tiers.** PRIMARY: search, type filter, card click (AS-03/05/08). SECONDARY: folder scope, pick mode bar, Upload, Stock, Icons (AS-04/09/16/22/32/33). ADVANCED: select mode + bulk (AS-12/13/14), detail hub rows (AS-24…31). CONTEXTUAL: quota band, local-only warning, oversized replacement, status pill (AS-18/19/20/21). Could-be-contextual: select mode via right-click is undiscoverable (G3-011).

- **Conditional UX.** Upload → size / type rule → refusal copy (code) vs Figma "Upload failed" overlay (G3-015) · empty library → Browse stock CTA both sides · quota ≥ 80 % → band, 100 % → upload off (both) · server unreachable → "First 200 / couldn't reach" scope line (both).
- **Consistency.** Figma board names split "Media · …" (drawer) and "Assets · …" (library / pick mode) for one family; code calls the panel "Assets" and the module `media` — pick "Assets" everywhere (boards + copy). Delete toast: Figma no Undo, code Undo (G3-013). Size copy 10 MB vs 50 MB (G3-061/062).

### C2 · Asset library

- **Tiers.** PRIMARY: search, grid / list, card select, Insert to canvas (AS-38/51/56/61). SECONDARY: folders + smart folders, tags filter, sort, upload / URL / stock doors (AS-39/40/41/44/45/47/52). ADVANCED: select files + bulk, versions, replace across site, rename, move (AS-53/59/60/62/63/70). CONTEXTUAL: drag-to-folder, status bar warnings (AS-49/67). Could-be-contextual: tags editing (code-only, G3-049).

- **Consistency.** Typed-DELETE guard exists for asset bulk >20 (code) but Figma reserves typed DELETE for site / collection / record / token — align: typed gate only when the action is irreversible *and* wide (record with page, collection, token in use, site). Per-file board cloning (10× rename, 10× delete, 15× details) inflates the family without adding states (G3-048/052/053).

### G3

### E1 · Owner decisions

1. **Which Brand design is the product?** Live Figma = full-screen Brand workspace (`7315:80955`); the plan's Q5 = Simple / Advanced *drawer* (now parked); code = a third drawer. The rail now opens a full-canvas view for Brand while Assets (quick chooser) and CMS (Q6) keep a drawer + full-canvas pair. Options: (a) workspace wins → update Q5, hide two parked designs, code rebuild = full page; (b) Q5 wins → restore `4428:143804…` to live, hide the workspace, code rebuild = drawer. Not invented here; affects 39 BR rows + 8 IN rows.
2. **Brand save model.** Code stages edits → Review N changes → Apply (with a review modal that lists only 3 of 14 kinds, BR-63). Workspace = every edit "saved to the draft" (`7318:81119`), no Save, confirm on Discard. Auto-draft matches the Assets / CMS "saves immediately" pattern; the review step protects against 34-element repaints. Pick one.
3. **Typed-DELETE policy.** Figma: site, collection, record, token. Code: assets >20 only. Proposed rule (C2): typed gate when the action is irreversible *and* wide (record with generated page · collection · token in use · site); plain confirm otherwise. Needs a yes.
4. **Export scope.** Figma `4418:97069` says "current page only (Home) · 1 file"; code exports every live page (ZIP) or the current page (HTML). Confirm the copy is a sample, not a scope change.
5. **Delete token: block (Figma in-use lock `4418:173732`) or replace (code `TokenReplaceModal`)?** Code's replace-then-delete is the safer product behaviour; the lock leaves 34 elements stranded. Recommend code behaviour + Figma copy change; also decide §15-4 (per-token dialog binding).
6. **Conditions (AS-91):** keep as a CMS DATA row (add to `4428:140486`, drop "Soon") or hide the shipped feature. It is SHIPPED and has canvas effect.
7. **Integrations screen:** keep 13 design-only boards under an `AUDIT · NOT IMPLEMENTED` card, or reduce to one "COMING SOON" list board matching code until OAuth exists.
8. **Members / Billing:** in-editor summary modal (Figma) vs direct dashboard link (code).
9. **Forms provider (`4418:131840` Formspree card, Soon) vs the unmounted `FormSettingsSection` (per-form, AS-98):** site-level or per-form routing — decide before either is built.
10. **Swatch picker palette (`7318:80959`, `4428:149324`) contains `#7E3AF2`** while Brand checks and DESIGN.md ban purple / violet: drop the swatch or exempt it (PRO / avatar tones only per DESIGN.md).
